o jˈeros ke i θalˈassa 

ˌitan ˌenas ˌilicomˈenos pu psˈarevˌe mˈonos tu se ˌena scˈif sto (en)ɡˈʌlf stɹˈiːm(el) ke aftˈos

ˈetreksˌe kalˈa stis plevrˈes tu prosˈopu tu ke ta çˈeriˌa tu ˌixan vˌaθiˈes ptiçˈes

ˌitan frˈeska ˌitan dˈoso paʎˈa ˈoso i ðˌiavrˈosis se mɲa ˈerimˌo xorˈiz psˈariˌa 

ta pˈada pˈano tu ˌitam baʎˈa ektˈos apˌo ta mˈatiˌa tu ke ˌitan ˈiðja 
xrˈoma sˈan di θˈalassˌa ke ˌitan ˌevðiˈaθetˌi ke aˈititˌi 

sˌadiˈaɣo tu ˈipe to aɣˈori kaθˈos anˈevenˌan stin ˈoxθi apˌo ˈopu to

θˈalassˌa

polˈi arɣˈa janˌa prˌospaθˈiso jˈa ðˈinamˌi tˈora mˈeso tis trofˈis ˌise ilˈiθiˌos 
ˈipe ston ˌeaftˈo tu fˈae to ˈalo iptˈamenˌo psˈari 

ˌitan ecˈi kˌaθarismˈeno ke ˈetimˌo ke to sˈikosˌe me to ˌaristerˈo tu çˈeri 
ke to ˈefajˌe masˈodas ta kˈokalˌa prˌosektikˈa ke trˈoɣodˌas to ˈolo mˈexri to 
urˈa 

ˌeçi pˌerissˈoterˌi trofˈi apˌo sçeðˈon ˌopioðˈipotˌe psˈari scˈefticˌe tulˈaçistˌon do 
ˈiðoz ðˈinamˌis pu xrçˈazomˌe tˈora ˈekanˌa ˌomikrontˈonos tˈi borˈo scˈefticˌe 
ˈas arçˈisi na kˈani cˈiklus ke na ˈerθi o aɣˈonas 

o ˈiʎos anˈetilˌe jˈa trˈiti forˈa apˌo tˈote pu ˌiçe vˈali sti θˈalassˌa ˈotan ˈomikrˌon 
ta psˈari ˈarçisˌan na kˈanuŋ ɡˈiklus 

ðˈen borˈuse na ðˈi apˌo tiŋ ɡlˈisi tis pˌetoɲˈas ˈoti to psˈari ˈekanˌe cˈiklus 
ˌitam bolˈi norˈiz jˈa aftˈo aplˈos ˈeɲosˌe mɲ ˌamiðrˈi xalˈarosˌi tu 
pˈiesˌi tis pˌetoɲˈas ke ˈarçisˌe na tin dravˈai ˌapalˈa me to ðikˈo tu 
ðeksˈi çˈeri ˈesfiksˌe ˈopos pˈada alˌa mˈolis ˈeftasˌe sto 
sto simˈio pu θa ˈespajˌe ˈarçisˌe na bˈeni ɣramˈi ɣlˈistrisˌe ti ðicˈi tu 
tus ˈomus ke to cefˈali apˌo kˈato apˌo ti ɣramˈi ke ˈarçisˌe na travˈai sti ɣramˈi 
stˌaθerˈa ke ˌapalˈa xrˌisimopˈiisˌe ke ta ðˈio tu çˈeriˌa se mɲa ˌeorˈumenˌi cˈinisˌi 
ke prospˈaθisˌe na kˈani to trˈaviɣmˌa ˈoso pˌerissˈoterˌo borˈuse me to sˈoma tu ke to ðikˈo tu 
pˈoðja ta paʎˈa tu pˈoðja ke i ˈomi pˌeristrˈefodˌan me tin eˈorisˌi tu 
travˈodas 

ˌine ˌenas polˈi meɣˈalos cˈiklos ˈipe alˌa kˈani cˈiklus 

tˈote i ɣramˈi ðˈen ˈebenˌe ˈalo ke tiŋ ɡrˈatisˌe mˈexri na ðˈi 
i staɣˈones pu piðˈun apˌo aftˈo ston ˈiʎo metˈa ksecˈinisˌe ke to paʎˈo 
o ˈadraz ɣonˈatisˌe ke to ˈafisˌe na pˈai ðˌistaktikˈa pˈiso sto skˌotinˈo nerˈo 

kˈani to pçˈo mˌakrinˈo mˈeros tu cˈiklu tu tˈora ˈipe prˈepi na kratˈiso 
ˌomikrontˈonos tˈi borˈo scˈefticˌe to stˈelexˌos θa sˌidomˈevi toŋ ɡˈiklo tu kˈaθe forˈa 
ˈisos se mɲa ˈora na ton ðˈo tˈora prˈepi na tom bˈiso ke metˈa eɣˈo 
prˈepi na ton skotˈosi 

alˌa to psˈari sinˈeçizˌe na kˈani toŋ ɡˈiklo tu arɣˈa ke o jˈeros ˌitan mˈuscem apˌo ton iðrˈota 
ke kurˈasticˌe vˌaθiˈa sta kˈokalˈa tu ðˈio ˈores arɣˈoterˌa alˌa i cˈikli ˌitan 
polˈi pçˈo kodˈo tˈora ke apˌo ton drˈopo pu i ɣramˈi ˌitan loksˈi borˈuse na kˌatalˈavi to 
ta psˈariˌa ˌixan sˌikoθˈi stˌaθerˈa enˈo ecˈinos kˌolibˈuse 

jˈa mɲa ˈora o jˈeros ˈevlepˌe mˈavra stˈiɣmatˌa brostˈa sta mˈatiˌa tu ke 
o iðrˈotas alˈatisˌe ta mˈatiˌa tu ke alˈatisˌe to kˈopsimˌo sto mˈati ke sto ðikˈo tu 
mˈetopˌo ðˈen fovˈotan da mˈavra stˈiɣmatˌa ˌitaŋ ɡˌanonicˈi sto 
i ˈedasˌi pu travˈuse sti ɣramˈi ðˈio forˈes ˈomos ˌiçe 
ˈeɲoθˌe lˌipoθimˈia ke zalˈaða ke aftˈo ton ˌiçe ˌanisiçˈisi

ˌekatˈo patˈeres imˈon ke ˌekatˈo çˈere marˈies alˌa ðˈen borˈo na ta pˈo 
tˈora 

sceftˈite ˈoti ˈipan scˈefticˌe θa ta pˈo arɣˈoterˌa 

ˌakrivˈos tˈote ˈeɲosˌe ˌena ksˌafnikˈo xtˈipimˌa ke trˈadaɣmˌa sti ɣramˈi pu kratˈuse 
ta ðˈio tu çˈeriˌa ˌitan ˌexmirˈo ke sklirˈo ke varˈi 

xtipˈai me to ðˈori tu ton sirmˈatinˌo ijˈeti scˈefticˌe aftˈo ˌitan 
ˌanaŋɡasmˈenoz na ˈerθi ˈeprepˌe na to kˈani aftˈo borˈi ˈomoz na toŋ ɡˈani na piðˈiksi ke eɣˈo 
θa prˌotimˈuse na mˈini na kˈani cˈiklus tˈora t ˈalmatˌa ˌitan ˌaparˈetitˌa jˈa aftˈon 
na pˈari aˈera alˌa metˈa apˌo aftˈo to kaθˈena borˈi na ðˌievrˈini to ˈaniɣmˌa tu ɣˈadzu

mˈim biðˈaz psˈari ˈipe mˈim biðˈas 

to psˈari xtipˈuse to sˈirma polˈes forˈes pˌerissˈoterˌo ke kˈaθe forˈa kunˈuse to ðikˈo tu 
cefˈali o jˈeros parˈatisˌe mɲa mikrˈi ɣramˈi 

prˈepi na kratˈiso tom bˈono tu ecˈi pu ˌine scˈefticˌe to ðikˈo mu ðˈen ˌeçi sˌimasˈia iˈota 
borˈi na elˈeɣksi to ðikˈo mu alˌa o pˈonos tu θa borˈuse na ton drelˈani 

metˈa apˌo lˈiɣo to psˈari stamˈatisˌe na xtipˈai sto sˈirma ke ˈarçisˌe na kˈani cˈiklus 
siɣˈa siɣˈa pˈali o jˈeros cˈerðizˌe ɣramˈi stˌaθerˈa plˈeon ˈomos ˈeɲoθˌe 
lˌipoθimˈisi ksanˈa sˈikosˌe me to ˌaristerˈo tu çˈeri lˈiɣo θˌalassinˈo nerˈo ke to fˈoresˌe 
to cefˈali tu metˈa fˈoresˌe cˈi ˈalo ke ˈetripsˌe to pˈiso mˈeros tu lemˈu tu 

ðˈen ˌexo krˈabes ˈipe θa sˌikoθˈi sˈidomˌa ke borˈo n adˈekso esˈi 
prˈepi na ðˌiarcˈesi mˈin do milˈas kˈan 

ɣonˈatisˌe sto tˈokso ke jˈa mɲa stiɣmˈi ɣlˈistrisˌe ti ɣramˈi pˈano apˌo to ðikˈo tu 
pˈali pˈiso θa ksˌekurastˈo tˈora ˈoso θa vjˈi stoŋ ɡˈiklo ke metˈa 
sˌikoθˈite ke ðulˈepste ton ˈotan bˈi ˌapofˈasisˌe 

ˌitan meɣˈalos pˌirasmˈoz na ksˌekurastˈis stim blˈori ke n afˈisis to psˈari na ftiˈaksi ˌena 
cˈikloz mˈonos tu xorˈiz n ˌanaktˈisi kamˈia ɣramˈi ˈotan ˈomos to stˈelexˌos 
ˈeðiksˌe ˈoti to psˈari ˌiçe jirˈisi janˌa ˈerθi prˈos ti vˈarka o jˈeros sikˈoθicˌe 
ta pˈoðja tu ke ˈarçisˌe to strofˈarismˌa ke to trˈaviɣmˌa tis ˈifansˌis pu ˈeferˌe

ˌanevˈeni al aftˈo θa ˌine kalˈo na tom bˈari mazˌi tu to xrçˈazomˌe 
kakˈos 

θa ksˌekurastˈo stin epˈomenˌi strofˈi kaθˈos θa vjˈi ˈipe ɲˈoθo polˈi 
kalˈiterˌa metˈa se ðˈiotrˈis strofˈes akˈoma θa ton ˌexo

ˌipoklˈiθicˌe me to trˈaviɣmˌa tis pˌetoɲˈas kaθˈos ˈeɲosˌe to psˈari na jirˈizi 

ðulˈevis tˈora psˈari scˈefticˌe θa se pˈao sti strofˈi

ˈeprepˌe na to ˌeçi janˌa jirˈisi spˈiti 

θa kˌatefθˈino mˈono nˈotiˌa ke ðˌitikˈa ˈipe ˌenas ˈanθropˌoz ðˈen xˈanetˌe potˈe sti θˈalassˌa 
ke ˌine makrˈi nisˈi 

ˌitan stin drˈiti strofˈi pu ˈiðe prˈotos to psˈari

vˈarka pu ðˈen borˈuse na pistˈepsi to mˈikos tis 

ˈoçi ˈipe ðˈen borˈi na ˌine tˈoso meɣˈalos 

alˌa ˌitan dˈoso meɣˈalos ke sto tˈelos aftˈu tu cˈiklu ˈeftasˌe sto 
ˌepifˈaɲa mˈolis triˈada mˈetra mˌakriˈa ke o ˈadras ˈiðe tin urˈa tu ˈekso apˌo to nerˈo 
ˌitam bzilˈoter apˌo mɲa meɣˈali lepˈiða ðrepˈani ke mɲa polˈi xlomˈi levˈad apˌo pˈano 
to skˈuro blˈe nerˈo jˈirisˌe pˈiso ke kaθˈos ta psˈariˌa kolˈibisˌan ˌakrivˈos kˈato apˌo to 
ˌepifˈaɲa o jˈeroz borˈuse na ðˈi ton derˈastiˌo ˈoŋɡo tu ke tiz mˈov rˈijes pu

fˌarðiˈa 

se aftˈon doŋ ɡˈiklo o jˈeroz borˈuse na ðˈi to mˈati tu psˌariˈu ke ta ðˈio ɡrˈiza

se 

alˌa prˈepi na tom bˈaro kodˈa kodˈa kodˈa scˈefticˌe ðˈem brˈepi na prˌospaθˈiso 
to cefˈali prˈepi na pˈaro tiŋ ɡˌarðiˈa 

na ˌise ˈiremˌos ke ðˌinatˈoz jˈerodˌa ˈipe 

ston epˈomenˌo cˈiklo i plˈati tu psˌariˈu ˌitan ˈekso alˌa ˌitan lˈiɣo polˈi mˌakriˈa 
apˌo to skˈafos ston epˈomenˌo cˈiklo ˌitan akˈoma polˈi mˌakriˈa alˌa ˌitan 
psilˈoterˌa ˈekso apˌo to nerˈo ke o jˈeros ˌitan sˈiɣurˌos ˈoti cerðˈizodˌaz lˈiɣo pˌerissˈoterˌo 
ɣramˈi pu θa borˈuse na ton ˌeçi ðˈipla tu 

ˌiçe ˌarmatˈosi to kamˈaci tu polˈi prˈin ke to pinˈio apˌo ˌelafrˈi sçinˈi ˌitan mˌesa 
ˌena strˌoŋɡilˈo kalˈaθi ke to telˈiomˌa jinˈotan ɣrˈiɣorˌa sto bˈit stim blˈori 

to psˈari ˈebenˌe stoŋ ɡˈiklo tu tˈora ˈiremˌo ke ˈomorfˌo ke 
mˈono i meɣˈali tu urˈa cinˈite o jˈeros trˈaviksˌe pˈano tu ˈolo aftˈo 
borˈuse na ton fˈeri pçˈo kodˈa jˈa mɲa stiɣmˈi to psˈari jˈirisˌe lˈiɣo 
sto plevrˈo tu metˈa ˈisjosˌe ke ˈarçisˌe ˌenan ˈalo cˈiklo 
ton mˌetacˈinisˌa ˈipe o jˈeros ton mˌetacˈinisˌa tˈote 

ˈeɲosˌe pˈali lˌipoθimˈia tˈora alˌa krˈatisˌe to meɣˈalo psˈari ˈoli tim bˈiesˌi 
ˈoti borˈuse ton siŋɡˈinisˌa scˈefticˌe ˈisos aftˈi ti forˈa tom bˈaro 
telˈiosˌe trˈava çˈeriˌa scˈefticˌe sˌikoθˈite pˈoðja tˌeleftˈeo jˈa mˈena cefˈali 
tˌeleftˈea jˈa mˈena ðˈem bˈijes potˈe aftˈi ti forˈa θa ton dravˈikso apˌo pˈano 

alˌa ˈotan ˈevalˌe ˈoli tu tim brospˈaθça ksˌecinˈodas tim bolˈi prˈin apˌo to 
psˈariˌa ˈirθe ðˈipla ke travˈodaz me ˈoli tu ti ðˈinamˌi to psˈari trˈaviksˌe 
telˈiosˌe lˈiɣo ke metˈa lˈijisˌe ke kolˈibisˌe mˌakriˈa 

psˈari ˈipe o jˈeroz psˈari θa prˈepi na peθˈanis ˈutos i ˈalos 
prˈepi na me skotˈosis cˈi emˈena 

ˈetsi ðˈen jˈinetˌe tˈipotˌa scˈefticˌe to stˈoma tu ˌitam bolˈi steɣnˈo 
mˈil alˌa ðˈen borˈuse na pçˈasi to nerˈo tˈora prˈepi na tom bˈaro 
parˈalilˌa me aftˈi ti forˈa scˈefticˌe ðˈen ˌime kalˈoz jˈa polˈes akˈomi strofˈes 
nˈe ˌise ˈipe ston ˌeaftˈo tu ˌise kalˈoz jˈa pˈada 

stin epˈomenˌi strofˈi sçeðˈon don ˌiçe alˌa ke pˈali to psˈari ðˌiorθˈoθicˌe 
ton ˌeaftˈo tu ke kolˈibisˌe arɣˈa mˌakriˈa 

me skotˈoniz psˈari scˈefticˌe o jˈeros alˌa ˌeçiz ðicˈeomˌa 
na potˈe ðˈen ˌexo ðˈi mˌeɣalˈiterˌo i pçˈo ˈomorfˌo i pçˈo ˈiremˌo ˌita tˈonos 
pçˈo ˌevjenikˈo prˈaɣm apˌo sˈen ˌaðerfˈe ˈela ke skˈotosˌe me ðˈen do kˈano 
ɲˈazetˌe pˈios skotˈoni pˈion 

tˈora berðˈevesˌe sto cefˈali scˈefticˌe prˈepi na kratˈisis 
to cefˈali su kˌaθarˈo krˈata to cefˈali su kˌaθarˈo ke ksˈeriz na ˌipofˈeris sˈan ˈalfa 
ˈanθropˌos i ˌena psˈari scˈefticˌe 

kaθˈarisˌe cefˈali ˈipe me mɲa fonˈi pu metˈa vˈias ˈakujˌe ksˌekaθarˈizo 

ˈalez ðˈio forˈes ˌitan do ˈiðiˌo stis strofˈes 

ðˈeŋ ɡzˈero scˈefticˌe o jˈeros ˌitan sto simˈio tu 
ɲˈoθodˌas ton ˌeaftˈo tu na fˈevji kˈaθe forˈa ðˈeŋ ɡzˈero θa to ðˌocimˈaso ˈomoz mɲa forˈa 
pˌerissˈoterˌa 

to ðocˈimasˌe ˈali mɲa forˈa ke ˈeɲosˌe ton ˌeaftˈo tu na pijˈeni ˈotan jˈirisˌe to 
psˈariˌa to psˈari ðˌiorθˈoθicˌe ke kolˈibisˌe ksanˈa arɣˈa me to 
meɣˈali urˈa ˈifansˌi ston aˈera 

θa to ksˌanaðˌocimˈaso ˌiposçˈeθicˌe o jˈeros an ke ta çˈeriˌa tu ˌitan vˌurkomˈena 
tˈora ke ˈevlepˌe kalˈa mˈono sta flˈas 

to ksˌanaðocˈimasˌe ke ˌitan do ˈiðiˌo ˈetsi scˈefticˌe ke ˈeɲosˌe

pˈire ˈolo tu tom bˈono ke ˌomikrontˈonos tˈi ˌiçe ˌapomˈini apˌo tiz ðinˈamis tu ke to makrˈi tu 
ˈefijˌe to kamˈari ke to ˈevalˌe enˈadiˌa stin ˌaɣonˈia tu psˌariˈu ke to psˈari ˈirθe 
pˈerasˌe sto plˈaɪ tu ke kolˈibisˌe ˌapalˈa sto plˈaɪ tu sçeðˈon o lˌoɣarˌiasmˈos tu 
aŋɡˈizodˌas to sanˈiði tu scˈif ke ˈarçisˌe na pernˈai ti vˈarka mˌakriˈa 
vaθˈi farðˈi ˌasimˈi ke fraɣmˈeno me mˈov ke ˌatelˈiotˌo sto nerˈo 

o jˈeros ˈeriksˌe ti ɣramˈi ke ˈevalˌe to pˈoði tu pˈano tis ke sˈikosˌe to 
kamˈaci ˈoso pçˈo psilˈa borˈuse ke to ˈeðiˌokse me ˈoli tu ti ðˈinamˌi 
ke pˌerissˈoterˌi ðˈinamˌi ˌiçe mˈolis kalˈesi stim blevrˈa tu psˌariˈu ˌakrivˈos

ˌaristerˈa apˌo aftˈon i mˌiroðiˈa tu ˈematˌos apˌo ta çˈeriˌa mu ðˈen simˈeni tˈipotˌa tˈora me ˈola
